where

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap{}}
is the
merge operator for session types
(
full merging
):

\small\begin{array}[]{c}\textstyle{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqcap}}\!{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{j}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{j}}\right\}_{\!j\in J}}\\
\;=\;{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{k}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}^{\prime}_{k}}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqcap}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{k}})\right\}_{k\in I\cap J}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I\setminus J}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{j}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{j}}\right\}_{\!j\in J\setminus I}}\\[8.53581pt]
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\,\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqcap}}\,{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\;=\;{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}^{\prime}_{i}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqcap}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{i}})\right\}_{i\in I}}\\[2.84526pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}}}\,\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqcap}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}}\,=\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqcap}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T})}}\hskip 18.49988pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}\,\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqcap}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}\,=\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}\hskip 18.49988pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\,\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqcap}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\,=\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\end{array}

We parameterise our theory on a (fixed) set of
reliable
roles

{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
, i.e. roles assumed to
never crash
, for modelling optional reliability assumptions:
if
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}=\emptyset
, we assume every role to be unreliable and
susceptible to crash;
if
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}\subseteq{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
, we assume every role in
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
to be
reliable, and we simulate the results from the original MPST theory without
crashes
2
2

2

Here we consider the original MPST theory without delegation (session
passing).

.
We base our definition of projection on the standard
definition
[
POPL19LessIsMore
]
, but include more (
highlighted
) cases to account for reliable
roles,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}
branches, and runtime global types.

When projecting a transmission from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
to
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}
, we remove the

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}
label from the internal choice at
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
, reflecting our
model that a
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}
‘pseudo’-message cannot be sent.
Dually, we require a
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}
label to be present in the external choice
at
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}
– unless the sender role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
is assumed to be reliable.
Our definition of projection enforces that transmissions, whenever
an unreliable role is the sender (
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\notin{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
),

must include
a crash handling branch
(
\exists k\in I:{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{k}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}
).
This requirement ensures that the receiving role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}
can
always
handle
crashes whenever it happens, so that processes are not stuck when crashes occur.
We explain how these requirements help us achieve various properties by
projection, such as safety, deadlock-freedom, and liveness, in Section
4.6
.
The rest of the rules, including the definition of the merge operator, are
taken from the literature
[
POPL19LessIsMore
,
VanGlabbeekLICS2021
]
,
without much modification.

Subtyping

We define a
subtyping
relation
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}
on local types in
subsection 4.1
,
which will be used later to relate the semantics of global types and configurations in Section
4.5
.
Our subtyping relation is mostly standard
[
POPL19LessIsMore
, Def. 2.5]
,
except for the (
highlighted
) addition of the rule
[Sub-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}
]
and
extra requirements in
[Sub-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}
]
.
In
[Sub-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}
]
, we add two additional requirements:

(1)
the supertype cannot be a ‘pure’ crash handling branch;
and
(2)
if the subtype has a crash handling branch, then the supertype
must also have one.
For simplicity, we do not consider subtyping on basic types
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}
.

{defi}

[Subtyping]

The subtyping relation
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}
is coinductively defined:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\quad\par{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I\cup J}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\lx@proof@logical@and\forall i\in I{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{i}}\mathchoice{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\displaystyle\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}\,\middle|\,{k\in I}\right\}}}\neq\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}\right\}}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\textstyle\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}\,\middle|\,{k\in I}\right\}}}\neq\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}\right\}}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptstyle\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}\,\middle|\,{k\in I}\right\}}}\neq\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}\right\}}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptscriptstyle\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}\,\middle|\,{k\in I}\right\}}}\neq\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}\right\}}}$}}\mathchoice{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\displaystyle\nexists j\in J:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\textstyle\nexists j\in J:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptstyle\nexists j\in J:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptscriptstyle\nexists j\in J:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$}}\par\par\\[8.61108pt]
\mathchoice{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\textstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$}}\quad\par{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I\cup J}}\lx@proof@logical@and\forall i\in I{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{i}}\par\\[8.61108pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}{}\mathord{\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}}}\right\}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}\quad\par{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}{}\mathord{\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}}}\right\}}\end{array}

As standard, our subtyping relation is reflexive and transitive.
Additionally, the properties of subtyping related to merges are
demonstrated in Lemmas
3
,
4
,
and
5
. The proofs are available in Appendix
.

Lemma 2

(Reflexivity and Transitivity of Subtyping)
.

The subtyping relation
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}
is reflexive and transitive.

Lemma 3
.

Given a collection of mergable local types
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}
(
i\in I
).
For all
j\in I
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{j}}
holds.

Lemma 4
.

Given a collection of mergable local types
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}
(
i\in I
).
If for all
i\in I
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}
for some local type
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}
,
then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}}}
.

Lemma 5
.

Given two collections of mergable local types
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}
(
i\in I
).
If for all
i\in I
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}
, then

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}_{i}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}}}
.

4.2.
Crash-Stop Semantics of Global Types

We now give a Labelled Transition System (LTS) semantics to global types,
with crash-stop semantics.
To this end, we first introduce some
auxiliary definitions.
We define the transition labels in
subsection 4.2
,
which are also used in the LTS semantics of configurations
(later in Section
4.3
).

{defi}

[Transition Labels]
Let
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}
be a transition label of the form:

\begin{array}[]{rclllll}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}&\Coloneqq&{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\!:\!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}}&(\text{${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}$ receives ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}$ from ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$})&\mathbin{\;\big|\;}&{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\!:\!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}}&(\text{${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}$ sends ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}$ to ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$})\\
&\mathbin{\;\big|\;}&{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}&(\text{${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}$ crashes})&\mathbin{\;\big|\;}&{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathord{\odot}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}&(\text{${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}$ detects the crash of ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$})\end{array}

The subject of a transition label, written

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subj}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}})}
,  is defined as:

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subj}({{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\!:\!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subj}({{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\!:\!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subj}({{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subj}({{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathord{\odot}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}.

The labels

{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\!:\!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}}

and

{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\!:\!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}}

describe sending and receiving actions respectively.
The crash of a role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
is denoted by the label

{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}
, and
the detection of a crash by label
{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathord{\odot}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}
:
we model crash detection at
reception
, the label
contains a
detecting
role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
and a
crashed
role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
.
We use these labels when giving the semantics for global types and
configurations.

We then define an operator to
remove
a role from a
global type in
subsection 4.2
: the intuition is to remove any interaction of a
crashed role from the given global type.
When a role has crashed, we remove it by attaching a
crashed annotation
,
and removing infeasible actions, e.g. when the sender and receiver of a
transmission have both crashed.
The removal operator is a partial function that takes a global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
and a
live role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}
(
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
) and gives a global type

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}
.

{defi}

[Role Removal]
The removal of a live role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
in a global type

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
, written
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}
,  is defined as follows:

\small\begin{array}[]{@{}r@{~}c@{~}l@{}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}})}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}&=&\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{}ll@{}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}})}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}&\text{if~}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\text{~and~}\exists j\in I:{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}})}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}&\text{if~}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\\
\makebox[123.80064pt][l]{${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}})}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}$
}&\text{if~}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\text{~and~}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\end{array}\right.\\[22.76219pt]
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}})}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}&=&\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{}ll@{}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}})}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}&\text{if~}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\\
\makebox[123.80064pt][l]{${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}$ ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}$ ${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}$ }&\text{if~}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}})}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}&\text{if~}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\text{~and~}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\end{array}\right.\\[22.76219pt]
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}})}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}&=&\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{}ll@{}}\makebox[123.80064pt][l]{${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}$ ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}$ ${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}$ }&\text{if~}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\text{~and~}\exists j\in I:{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}})}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}&\text{if~}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\text{~and~}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\end{array}\right.\\[17.07164pt]
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}})}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}&=&\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{}ll@{}}\makebox[123.80064pt][l]{${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}$ ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}$ ${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}$ }&\text{if~}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}})}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}&\text{if~}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\text{~and~}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\end{array}\right.\\[17.07164pt]
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}})}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}&=&\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{}ll@{}}\makebox[123.80064pt][l]{${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}})}}$ }&\text{if~}\operatorname{fv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}}\right)\neq\emptyset\text{~or~}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}})}\neq\emptyset\\
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}&\text{otherwise}\end{array}\right.\\[17.07164pt]
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}\hskip 113.81102pt{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}\end{array}

We now explain the definition in detail.
For simple cases, the removal of a role
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}
attaches
crash annotations
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}
on all occurrences of the removed role

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}
throughout global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
inductively.

We draw attention to some interesting cases:
when we remove the sender role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
from a transmission prefix

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\!\to\!{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}
,
the result is a ‘pseudo’-transmission en route prefix

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}\!\rightsquigarrow\!{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}:j}

where
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}
.
This enables the receiver
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
to ‘receive’ the special
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}

after the crash of
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
, hence triggering the crash handling branch.
Recall that our definition of projection requires that a crash handling branch
be present whenever a crash may occur (
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\notin{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
).

When we remove the sender role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
from a transmission en route prefix

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\!\rightsquigarrow\!{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}:j}
,
the result
retains
the index
j
that was selected by
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
,
instead of the index associated with
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}
handling.
This is crucial to our crash modelling: when a role crashes, the messages that
the role
has sent
to other roles are still available.
We discuss alternative models later in Section
4.4
.

In other cases, where removing the role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}
would render a transmission
(regardless of being en route or not) meaningless, e.g. both sender and receiver
have crashed, we simply remove the prefix entirely.

{exa}

We remove role
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}
in the global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
in Equation (
1
) (defined in Section
2
).

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}{\to}{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{trigger}}}}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C^{\lightning}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}{\to}{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{fatal}}}}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}}}}}}}}}}

Role
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}
now carries a crash annotation
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}
in the
resulting global type, denoting it has crashed.
Crash annotations change the reductions available for global types.

We now give a Labelled Transition System (LTS) semantics to a
global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
,
by defining the semantics with a tuple
\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle
,
where

{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}
is a set of
crashed
roles,
with all roles in
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}
belonging to the fixed role set
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathcal{R}}
, i.e.
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}\subseteq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathcal{R}}
.
The transition system is parameterised by reliability assumptions, in the form
of a fixed set of reliable roles
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
.
Where it is not ambiguous, we write

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
as an abbreviation of
\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\emptyset}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle
.
We define the reduction rules of global types in
subsection 4.2
.

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}}\rangle\lx@proof@logical@and\forall i\in I:\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}\rangle{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subj}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}})}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\end{array}

Figure 7
.
Global type reduction rules.

{defi}

[Global Type Reductions]

The global type (annotated with a set of crashed roles
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}
)
transition relation

\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}

is inductively
defined by the rules in
Figure

7
,
parameterised by a fixed set
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
of reliable roles.
We write

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{\phantom{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle

if there
exists
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}
such that

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
;
we write

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{\phantom{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}

if there
exists
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}
such that

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
,
and
\xrightarrow{}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}^{\!*}
for the transitive and reflexive closure of

\xrightarrow{\phantom{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}
.

Rules
[GR-
\oplus
]
and
[GR-
\&
]
model sending
and receiving messages respectively, as are standard in existing
works
[
ICALP13CFSM
]
.
We add a (
highlighted
) extra condition that the message exchanged not be a
‘pseudo’-message carrying the
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}
label.

[GR-
\mu
]
is a standard rule that deals with recursion.

We introduce (
highlighted
) rules to account for crash and consequential behaviour.

•

Rule
[GR-
\lightning
]
models crashes, where a live (
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
), but unreliable (
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
) role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
may crash.
The crashed role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
is added into the set of crashed roles (
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}\cup\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\right\}}}
), and removed
from the global type, resulting in a global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}
.

•

Rule
[GR-
\odot
]
is for
crash detection
, where a live
role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
may detect that
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
has crashed at reception,
and then continues with the crash handling continuation labelled
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}
.
This rule only applies when the message en route is a ‘pseudo’-message, since
otherwise a message rests in the queue of the receiver and can be received
despite the crash of the sender (cf.
[GR-
\&
]
).

•

Rule
[GR-
\lightning{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}
]
models the orphaning of a message sent from a
live role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
to a crashed role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
.
Similar to the requirement in
[GR-
\oplus
]
, we add the side
condition that the message sent is not a ‘pseudo’-message.

Finally, rules
[GR-Ctx-i]
and
[GR-Ctx-ii]

allow non-interfering reductions of (intermediate) global types
under prefix, provided that all of the continuations can be reduced by
that label.

Remark 6

(Necessity of
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}
in Semantics)
.

While we can obtain the set of crashed roles in any global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
via

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
, we need a separate
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}
for bookkeeping
purposes.

Let

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}\{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}}},\quad{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}}\}}
,
we can have the following reductions:

\langle{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\emptyset}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\emptyset}}\langle{\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\right\}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{:}\{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}}},\quad{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}}\}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}{}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\emptyset}}\langle{\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\right\}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}}\rangle

While we can deduce
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
is a crashed role in the interim global type,
the same information cannot be recovered from the final global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}
.

Both live and crashed roles in a global type remain consistent throughout a transition,
except for those directly involved in the crash transition action, as demonstrated in
7
.

Lemma 7

(No Revival Or Unexpected Crashes)
.

Assume

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
.

(1)

If
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}
, then
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
;

(2)

If
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}
, then
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
;

(3)

If
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}={{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}
, then
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
.

Proof 4.1
.

By induction on global type reductions. See Appendix
for details.

We introduce an auxiliary concept of
well-annotated
global
types in
subsection 4.2
, as a consistency requirement for crash
annotations
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}
in a global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
, and the set of crashed
roles
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}
, and a fixed set of reliable roles
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
.
We show that well-annotatedness w.r.t.
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
is preserved by global type
reductions in
8
.
It follows that, a global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
without runtime constructs is trivially
well-annotated, and all reducta

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}\xrightarrow{}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}^{\!*}{}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
are also well-annotated.

{defi}

[Well-Annotated Global Types]

A global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
with crashed roles
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}
is
well-annotated

w.r.t. a (fixed) set of reliable roles
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
, iff:

(WA1)

No reliable roles are crashed,
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}\cap{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}=\emptyset
;  and,

(WA2)

All roles with crash annotations are in the crashed set,

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}\subseteq{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}
;  and,

(WA3)

A role cannot be live and crashed simultaneously,
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}\cap{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}=\emptyset
.

Lemma 8

(Preservation of Well-Annotated Global Types)
.

If

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
,  and

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle
is well-annotated w.r.t.
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
, then

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
is also well-annotated w.r.t.
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
.

Proof 4.2
.

By induction on global type reductions (
subsection 4.2
).
See Appendix
for details.

4.3.
Crash-Stop Semantics of Configurations

After giving semantics to global types, we now give an
LTS semantics to
configurations
,
i.e. a collection of local types and communication queues across roles.
We first give a definition of configurations
in
subsection 4.3
, followed by their reduction rules in
subsection 4.3
.

{defi}

[Configurations]A configuration is a tuple
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
,  where

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
is a
typing context
, denoting a partial mapping
from roles to local types,
defined as:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\,\Coloneqq\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\emptyset}\mathbin{\;\big|\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\triangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}}

The
context composition

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{2}}

is defined iff
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}}\right)}\cap{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{2}}}\right)}=\emptyset
.
A typing context
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
can be
decomposed
(or
split
) into sub-contexts

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{2}}
, written
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{2}}
, if
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}}\right)}\cup{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{2}}}\right)}
,
and
\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{2}})\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}
.
We write
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}]}
for typing context updates,
namely

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}]}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}]}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}
(where
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
).

A
queue
, denoted
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}
, is either a (possibly empty) sequence of
messages

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{M}_{1}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{M}_{2}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\cdot}\cdots{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\cdot}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{M}_{n}}
,
or an unavailable queue
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}
.
We write
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}
for an empty queue, and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}M}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau^{\prime}}}}

for a non-empty queue with message
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}M}
at the beginning.
A
queue message

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}M}
is of form

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}
,
denoting a message with label
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}
and payload
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}
.
We sometimes omit
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}
when the payload is not of specific
interest.

We write
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
to denote a
queue environment
, a collection of
peer-to-peer queues.
A queue from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
to
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
at environment
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
is denoted

{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}})
.
We define queue environment updates
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}]}

similarly.

We also write
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau^{\prime}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}M}}}
for appending a message at the end
of a queue:
the message is appended to the sequence when
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau^{\prime}}
is available, or
discarded when
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau^{\prime}}
is unavailable
(i.e.
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}M}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}
).
Additionally, we write

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}

for
making all the queues to
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
unavailable: i.e.
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}_{1}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}_{2}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}\cdots{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}_{n}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}
.

We write
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}}}

to denote an
empty
queue environment,
where
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}},{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}})={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}
for any

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
in the domain.

We give an LTS semantics of configurations in
subsection 4.3
.
Similar to that of global types, we model the semantics of configurations
in an asynchronous (a.k.a. message-passing) fashion, using a queue environment to
represent the communication queues among all roles.

\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\,\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{k}}})}}}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{k}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{k}}})}}}]}\lx@proof@logical@and{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}k\in I\\[5.69054pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\,\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{k}}})}}}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{k}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau^{\prime}}]}\lx@proof@logical@and{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}k\in I{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{k}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau^{\prime}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}\\[5.69054pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\lx@proof@logical@and{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathord{\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}}}\right\}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\qquad\mathchoice{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}\lx@proof@logical@and{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\textstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}\lx@proof@logical@and{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}\lx@proof@logical@and{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}\lx@proof@logical@and{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$}}\\

.
Configuration semantics.

{defi}

[Configuration Semantics]The
configuration transition relation
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}

is defined in
Fig.

8
.
We write
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}
iff
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}
for some

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}

and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}
.  We define two
reductions

\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}
and

\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}
(where
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
is a fixed set of
reliable roles)
as follows.

•

We write
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}

for

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}

with

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\!\in\!\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}},{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}},{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathord{\odot}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}\right\}}}
.
We write

{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}

iff

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}

for some

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}
,  and

{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\!\not\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}
for its negation, and
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}{}^{\!\!\!*}}}
for the reflexive and transitive closure of
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}
;

•

We write

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}

for

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}

with

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\!\notin\!\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}\,\middle|\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\!\in\!{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\right\}}}
.
We write
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\!\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}
iff

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}

for some

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}
,
and

{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}}\!\not\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}

for its negation. We define

\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to^{*}_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}

as the reflexive and transitive closure of
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}
.

We first explain the standard rules:
rule
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}
]
(resp.
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}
]
)
says that a role can perform an output (resp. input) transition by appending
(resp. consuming) a message at the corresponding queue.
Recall that whenever a queue is unavailable, the resulting queue remains
unavailable after appending (
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}M}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}
).
Therefore, the rule
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}
]
covers delivery to both crashed
and live roles, whereas two separate rules are used in modelling global type
semantics (
[GR-
\oplus
]
and
[GR-
\lightning{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}
]
).
We also include a standard rule
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\mu
]
for recursive types.

The key innovations are the (
highlighted
) rules modelling crashes and crash
detection:
by rule
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\lightning
]
, a role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
may crash
and become
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}
at any time
(unless it is already
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}
ed or
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}
ped).
All of
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
’s receiving queues become unavailable
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}
, so that
future messages to
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
would be discarded.
Note that any pending messages in the receiving queue are discarded (since the
queue becomes unavailable
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}
),
since the messages cannot be processed after the crash.

Rule
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\odot
]
models crash detection and handling:
if
{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}
is crashed and stopped,
another role
{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}
attempting to receive from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}

can then take its
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}
handling branch. However, this rule only applies when the corresponding queue is empty: it is
still possible to receive messages sent before crashing via

[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}
]
.

4.4.
Alternative Modellings for Crash-Stop Failures

Before we dive into the relation between two semantics, let us have a short
digression to discuss our modelling choices and alternatives.
In this work, we mostly follow the assumptions laid out in

[
CONCUR22MPSTCrash
]
,
where a crash is detected at reception. However, they opt to use a synchronous
(rendez-vous) semantics, whereas we give an asynchronous (message-passing)
semantics, which entails interesting scenarios that would not arise in
a synchronous semantics.

Specifically,
consider the case where a role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
sends a message to
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
, and
then
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
crashes after sending, but before
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
receives the message.
The situation does not arise under a synchronous semantics, since sending and
receiving actions are combined into a single transmission action.

Intuitively, there are two possibilities to handle this scenario.
The questions are
whether the message sent immediately before crashing is deliverable to
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
,
and consequentially,
at what time
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
detects the crash of
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
.

In our semantics (
Figs.

7
and
8
), we
opt to answer the first question positively:
we argue that this model is more consistent with our ‘passive’ crash detection
design.
For example, if a role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
never receives from another role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
, then

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
does not need to react in the event of
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
’s crash.
Following a similar line of reasoning,
if the message sent by
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
arrives in the receiving queue of

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
,
then
