{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
should be able to receive the message, without triggering
a crash detection (although it may be triggered later).
As a consequence, we require in
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\odot
]
that the
queue
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}
be empty, to reflect the idea that
crash detection should be a ‘last resort’.

For an alternative model,
we can opt to detect the crash after it has occurred.
This is possibly better modelled with using outgoing queues
(cf.
[
FSTTCS15MPSTExpressiveness
]
),
instead of incoming queues in the semantics presented.
Practically, this may be the scenario that a TCP connection is closed (or
reset) when a peer has crashed,
and the content in the queue is lost.
It is worth noting that this kind of alternative model
will not affect our main theoretical results: the operational correspondence
between global and local type semantics, and furthermore, global type properties guaranteed by projection.

4.5.
Relating Global Type and Configuration Semantics

We have given LTS semantics for both global types
(
subsection 4.2
) and configurations (
subsection 4.3
).
We will now relate these two semantics with the help of the projection operator

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}}\upharpoonright{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{}}}}}
(
subsection 4.1
)
and the subtyping relation
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}
(
subsection 4.1
).

We associate configurations
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
with global types
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
(as
annotated with a set of crashed roles
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}
) by projection, written

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}
.
Naturally, there are two components of the association:
(1)
the local
types in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
need to correspond to the projections of the global type

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
and the set of crashed roles
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}
; and
(2)
the queues in

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
corresponds to the transmissions en route in the global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
and
also the set of crashed roles
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}
.

{defi}

[Association of Global Types and Configurations]

A configuration
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
is associated to a
(well-annotated w.r.t.
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
) global type

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle
,
written

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}
,
iff

(1)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
can be split into disjoint (possibly empty) sub-contexts

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}}
where:

(A1)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}
contains projections of
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
,  and

\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}}\right)}:\linebreak{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}
;

(A2)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}
contains crashed roles:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}
,
and

\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}
;

(A3)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}}
contains only
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}
endpoints:

\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}
.

(2)

(A4)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
is associated with global type

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle
,  given as follows:

(i)

Receiving queues for a role is unavailable if and only if
it has crashed:

\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}:\linebreak{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\in{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}\iff{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}
;

(ii)

If
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}
or
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}
,
then queues between all roles are empty (expect receiving queue
for crashed roles):

\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\notin{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}\implies{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}
;

(iii)

If

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}
,
or

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}
with
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}

(i.e. a ‘pseudo’-message is en route),
then

{enumerate*}
[label=
()
]

(iv)

if
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
is live, then the queue from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
to
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
is
empty:

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}\implies{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}
, and

(v)

\forall i\in I:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
is associated with

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}}\rangle
;

and,

(vi)

If

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}
with
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}
,
then

{enumerate*}
[label=
()
]

(vii)

the queue from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
to
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
begins with the message

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{j}}})}
:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{j}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}}}
;

(viii)

\forall i\in I:
removing the message from the head of the
queue,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}]}
is associated with

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}}\rangle
.

We write
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}
as an abbreviation of

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{\emptyset};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}
. We sometimes say a
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
(resp.
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
) is associated with

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle
for stating
Item

1

(resp.
Item

2
) is
satisfied.

We demonstrate the relation between the two semantics via association, by showing two
main theorems:
all possible reductions of a configuration have a corresponding action in
reductions of the associated global type (
9
);
and the reducibility of a global type is the same as its associated
configuration (
10
).

Theorem 9

(Completeness of Association)
.

Given associated global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
and configuration
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}
.
If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}
,
where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}

for all
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
,
then there exists
\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle}}

and

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
.

Proof 4.3
.

By induction on configuration reductions (
subsection 4.3
).
See Appendix
for details.

Theorem 10

(Soundness of Association)
.

Given associated global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
and configuration
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}
.
If

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}
,
then there exists
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}
and

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
,  such that

\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle}}
,
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}
.

Proof 4.4
.

By induction on global type reductions (
subsection 4.2
).
See Appendix
for details.

By Theorems
9
and
10
, we obtain, as a corollary, that
a global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
is in operational correspondence with the typing context

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\triangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}}\right\}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}}
,
which contains the projections of all roles in
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
.

Remark 11

(Sufficiency of Soundness Theorem)
.

Curious readers may wonder why we proved a soundness theorem that is not
the dual of the completeness theorem, e.g. as seen in the literature
[
ICALP13CFSM
]
.
This is a consequence of using ‘full’ subtyping (
subsection 4.1
, notably

[Sub-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}
]
).
A local type in the typing context may have fewer branches to choose from
than the projected local type,
resulting in uninhabited sending actions in the global type.

For example, let
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{1}}}}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}}};\;\;{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{2}}}}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}}}}\right\}}
.
An associated typing context
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
(assuming
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
reliable) may have

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}1}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}\right\}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}1}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}};\;\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}2}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}\right\}}
(via
[Sub-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}
]
).
The global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
may make a transition

{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{2}}}
, where an associated
configuration
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}}}
cannot.

Our soundness theorem is nevertheless
sufficient
for concluding
that desired properties are guaranteed via association, e.g. safety, deadlock-freedom, and liveness,
as illustrated in Section
4.6
.

Remark 12

(Relation between Well-Annotated and Well-Formed Global Types)
.

In the multiparty session type literature, a global type is
well-formed
if it can be projected onto
every declared protocol participant.
Readers may wonder how well-formedness is applied to the global type in this paper
and how well-annotated global types (
subsection 4.2
) relate to well-formed ones.
Our definition of association (
subsection 4.5
) ensures that a global type associated with a configuration,
with respect to a set of reliable roles
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
, is also well-formed with respect to
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
.
This is because:

{enumerate*}

every global type is closed, i.e. it has no free type variables;

every global type is contractive; and

condition (A1) in
subsection 4.5
ensures that the associated
global type is projectable onto all roles with respect to a set of reliable
roles, which is a key requirement for well-formedness.

Since the soundness (
10
) and completeness (
9
) of association, along with the results on typed session properties (discussed in Section
5
),
depend on the concept of association, it follows that all involved global types are well-formed.

Furthermore, there is no direct relationship between well-formedness and well-annotation; a well-annotated global type may not be well-formed, and vice versa. All main results apply to global types that are both well-formed and well-annotated.

4.6.
Properties Guaranteed by Projection

A key benefit of our top-down approach of multiparty protocol design is that
desirable properties are guaranteed by the methodology.
As a consequence, processes
following the local types obtained from projections are correct
by
construction
.
In this subsection, we focus on three properties:
communication safety
,

deadlock-freedom
, and
liveness
, and show that the
three properties are guaranteed from
configurations associated with global types.

Communication Safety

We begin by defining communication safety for configurations
(
subsection 4.6
).
We focus on the following two safety requirements:

{enumerate*}
[label=()]

each role must be able to handle any message that may end up in their
receiving queue (so that there are no label mismatches); and

each receiver must be able to handle the potential crash of the
sender, unless the sender is reliable.

{defi}

[Configuration Safety]
Given a fixed set of reliable roles
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
, we say that

\varphi
is an
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-safety property
of configurations iff, whenever
\varphi\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\right)
,  we have:

[S-
{\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}}{\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}}
]

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}

and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}

and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}

implies
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m^{\prime}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}}})}}}}}
;

[S-
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}{\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}}
]

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}

and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}

and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}

implies
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathord{\odot}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}}
;

[S-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu}
]

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}}}
implies
\varphi\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}\mathord{\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}}}\right\}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\right)
;

[S-
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to_{\!\lightning}}}
]

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}

implies
\varphi\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}}\right)
.

We say

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
is
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-safe
, if
\varphi\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\right)
holds for some
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-safety property
\varphi
.

We use a coinductive view of the safety property
[
SangiorgiBiSimCoInd
]
,
where the predicate of
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-safe configurations is the largest
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-safety property, by taking the union of all safety properties

\varphi
.
For a configuration
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
to be
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-safe, it has to satisfy
all clauses defined in
subsection 4.6
.

By clause
[S-
{\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}}{\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}}
]
, whenever a role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}

receives from another role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
,
and a message is present in the queue,
the receiving action must be possible for some label
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m^{\prime}}}
,
i.e. the receiver
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
must support all output messages that may appear
at the head of the queue sent from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
.

Clause
[S-
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}{\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}}
]
states that if a role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
receives
from a crashed role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
, and there is nothing in the queue,
then
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
must have a
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}
branch, and a crash detection action
can be fired.
(Note that
[S-
{\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}}{\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}}
]
applies when the queue is non-empty,
despite the crash of sender
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
.)

Finally,
clause
[S-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu}
]
extends the previous clauses
by unfolding any recursive entries; and
clause
[S-
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to_{\!\lightning}}}
]

states that any configuration
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}
which
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
transitions to
must also be
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-safe.
By using transition
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}
, we
ignore crash transitions
{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}
for
any reliable role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
.

{exa}

Recall the local types of the Simpler Logging example in Section
2
:

{\small{\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{read}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}}}\\[5.69054pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{report({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\operatorname{log}})}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\end{array}\hskip 18.49988pt\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{trigger}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}}\\[5.69054pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{fatal}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{read}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{report({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\operatorname{log}})}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\end{array}\right\}}\end{array}\\[17.07164pt]
\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{trigger}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}}\\[5.69054pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{read}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{read}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{report({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\operatorname{log}})}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{report({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\operatorname{log}})}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime\prime}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}}\end{array}\right\}}\\[11.38109pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime\prime}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{fatal}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\end{array}\end{array}}}

The configuration
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
, where

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}}\mathbin{\!\triangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}\mathbin{\!\triangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}\mathbin{\!\triangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}}}
, is

\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}},{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}\right\}}}
-safe.
This can be verified by checking its possible
reductions. For example,
in the case where
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}
crashes immediately, we have:

\begin{array}[]{@{}r@{}cl}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}&\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}\\
&\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{trigger}}}}}}}}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}]}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}},{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{trigger}}]}\\
&\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{trigger}}}}}}}}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}]}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}}]}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}\\
&\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathord{\odot}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}}}}}}}}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}]}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}}]}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime\prime}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}\\
&\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{fatal}}}}}}}}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}]}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}}]}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}},{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{fatal}}]}\\
&\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{fatal}}}}}}}}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}]}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}]}{}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}\end{array}

and each reductum satisfies all clauses of
subsection 4.6
.
The cases where
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}
crashes after sending the
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{read}}\text{ing}
message
to
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}
are similar. There are no other crash reductions to consider, since both

{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}
and
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}
are assumed to be reliable.
The cases where no crashes occur are similar as well,
except that
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\odot
]
and
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\lightning
]
are not applied in the non-crash reductions.

Deadlock-Freedom

The property of deadlock-freedom, sometimes also known as progress, describes
whether a configuration can keep reducing unless it is a terminal configuration.
We give its formal definition in
subsection 4.6
.

{defi}

[Configuration Deadlock-Freedom]
Given a set of reliable roles
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
, we say that a configuration
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
is

{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-deadlock-free
iff:

(1)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
is
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-safe; and,

(2)

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
can reduce to a configuration
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}

without further reductions:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to^{*}_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\not\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}
,  then:

(a)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
can be split into two disjoint contexts,
one with only
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}

entries, and one with only
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}
entries:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}
,  where

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}}}\right)}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\,\middle|\,{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}\right\}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}}\right)}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\,\middle|\,{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}}\right\}}}
;  and,

(b)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}
is empty for all pairs of roles, except for the receiving queues
of crashed roles, which are unavailable:

\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}

if

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}
,  and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}
,  otherwise.

It is worth noting that a (safe) configuration that reduces infinitely
satisfies deadlock-freedom, as
Item

2
in the premise does not hold.
Otherwise, whenever a terminal configuration is reached, it must satisfy

Item

2a
that all local types in the typing context be
terminated (either successfully
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}
, or crashed
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}
), and

Item

2b
that all queues be empty (unless unavailable due to
crash).
As a consequence,
a deadlock-free configuration
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
either does not stop reducing, or
terminates in a stable configuration.

Liveness

The property of liveness describes that every pending internal/external choice
is eventually triggered by means of a message transmission or crash detection.
Our liveness property is based on
fairness
,
which guarantees that every enabled message transmission, including crash detection,
is performed successfully.
We give the definitions of non-crashing, fair, and live paths of configurations respectively in
subsection 4.6
, and use these paths to
formalise the liveness for configurations in
subsection 4.6
.

{defi}

[Non-crashing, Fair, Live Paths]

A
non-crashing path
is a possibly infinite
sequence of configurations
({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{n}})_{n\in N}
,  where

N=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{0,1,2,\ldots\right\}}}

is a set of consecutive natural numbers, and

\forall n\in N
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{n}}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{n+1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{n+1}}
.

We say that a non-crashing path
({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{n}})_{n\in N}
is

fair
iff,
\forall n\in N
:

(F1)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{n}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}}}}}

implies
\exists k,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m^{\prime}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}}
such that
n\leq k\in N

and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{k}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m^{\prime}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}}})}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{k+1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{k+1}}
;

(F2)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{n}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}}}}}

implies
\exists k
such that
n\leq k\in N

and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{k}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{k+1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{k+1}}
;

(F3)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{n}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathord{\odot}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}}

implies
\exists k
such that
n\leq k\in N

and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{k}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathord{\odot}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{k+1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{k+1}}
.

We say that a non-crashing path
({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{n}})_{n\in N}

is
live
iff,
\forall n\in N
:

(L1)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{n}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}

implies
\exists k
such that
n\leq k\in N

and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{k}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{k+1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{k+1}}
;

(L2)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{n}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}
implies
\exists k,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m^{\prime}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}}
such that
n\leq k\in N
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{k}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m^{\prime}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}}})}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{k+1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{k+1}}

or

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{k}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{k}}\;\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathord{\odot}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{k+1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{k+1}}
.

A non-crashing path is a (possibly infinite) sequence of reductions of
a configuration without crashes. A non-crashing path is fair if along the path, every internal choice eventually
sends a message
(F1)
, every external choice eventually receives a message
(F2)
,
and every crash detection is eventually performed
(F3)
. A non-crashing path is live if
along the path, every non-crash message in the queue is eventually consumed
(L1)
, and every
hanging external choice eventually consumes a message or performs a crash detection
(L2)
.

{defi}

[Configuration Liveness]
Given a set of reliable roles
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
, we say that a configuration
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
is

{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-live
iff:

{enumerate*}
[leftmargin=*, nosep]

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
is
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-safe; and,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to^{*}_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}
implies all non-crashing paths starting with

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}
that are fair are also live.

A configuration
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
is
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-live when it is
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-safe and
any reductum of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
(via transition
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to^{*}_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}
)
consistently leads to a live path if it is fair.

{exa}

We illustrate safety, deadlock-freedom, and liveness over configurations via a series of small examples.
We consider the configuration
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A}}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A}}}
, where

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}

and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}}}
with:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\triangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}.{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ok}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ok}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ko}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\right\}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ko}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\right\}}}}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\triangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}.{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ok}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ok}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ko}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\right\}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ko}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ok}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\right\}}}}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\triangleright\!}{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ok}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\right\}}\right\}}}}\end{array}

If we assume that all roles are unreliable, i.e.
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}=\emptyset
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A}}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A}}}
is
\emptyset
-safe
since the inputs/outputs in the typing context
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A}}}
are dual and the queue environment
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A}}}
is empty.
However,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A}}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A}}}
is
neither

\emptyset
-deadlock-free
nor

\emptyset
-live since it is possible for
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
to crash immediately before
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
sends
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ko}}
to
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
. In such cases,
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
will
not
detect that
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
has crashed (since we only detect crashes on receive actions) and terminate
without
sending a message to the backup process
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}
. This results in a deadlock because
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}

will
detect that
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
has crashed, and
will
expect a message from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
.

We observe that changing the reliability assumptions, without changing the configuration, may influence whether a configuration property holds. For example, in the case of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A}}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A}}}
,
we
can
obtain liveness by adjusting the reliability assumptions: in fact, if we assume
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\in{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
, then

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A}}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}A}}}
is both
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-deadlock-free and
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-live.

Then consider the configuration
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}}}
, where

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}

and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}}}
with:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\triangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}.{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ok}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\triangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}.{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ok}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}.{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ok}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\right\}}}}\right\}}}}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\triangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}.{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ok}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}}}\end{array}

If we assume that all roles are unreliable, i.e.
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}=\emptyset
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}}}
is
\emptyset
-safe and
\emptyset
-deadlock-free but
not

\emptyset
-live – because

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
may never crash, and in this case,
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}
’s outputs are never received by
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
. Notice that, in the case of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}}}
, we are unable to make liveness hold purely via combinations of reliable roles: this is because (unless
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
crashes)
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}
’s output will never be received by

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}
, irrespective of reliability assumptions. The configuration itself must instead be adapted accordingly, e.g. in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}
should be permitted to send only once it has detected that
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
has crashed.

Finally, consider the configuration
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}C}}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}C}}}
, where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}C}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}C{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}C{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}C{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}C}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}}}
with:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}C{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\triangleright\!}{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}1}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}2}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}.{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ok}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}\right\}}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}C{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\triangleright\!}{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}1}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}2}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\right\}}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}C{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}&=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\triangleright\!}{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}.{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{ok}}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}\right\}}}}\right\}}}}\end{array}

When
all
roles are assumed to be reliable, i.e.
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\right\}}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}C}}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}C}}}
satisfies
\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\right\}}}
-safety and
\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\right\}}}
-deadlock-freedom. However, should we instead assume that no roles are reliable, i.e.
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}=\emptyset
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}C}}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}C}}}
satisfies only
\emptyset
-safety since external choices in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}C}}}
do not feature a crash handling branch when receiving from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}
.

Properties by Projection

We conclude by showing the guarantee of safety,
deadlock-freedom, and liveness in configurations
associated with global types in
13
.
Furthermore, as a corollary,
14
demonstrates that
a typing context projected from a global type (without runtime
constructs) is inherently safe, deadlock-free, and live by construction.

Lemma 13
.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}
,
then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}
is
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-safe,
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-deadlock-free, and
{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}
-live.

Proof 4.5
.

The results follow from the operational correspondence between
global types and configurations (Theorems
9

and
10
),
and the respective definitions for each property (Definitions
4.6
,

4.6
, and
4.6
).
See Appendix

for details.

5.
A Type System with Crash-Stop Semantics

In this section, we present a type system for our asynchronous multiparty session
calculus. Our typing system extends the one in
[
POPL21AsyncMPSTSubtyping
]
with
crash-stop failures – typing rules for crashed process and unavailable queues, respectively.
We introduce the typing rules in Section
5.1
, and show various
properties of typed sessions: subject reduction (
), session
fidelity (
), deadlock-freedom (
),
and liveness (
) in Section
.

5.1.
Typing Rules

Our type system involves four kinds of typing judgements:

(1)
for expressions;

(2)
for processes;

(3)
for queues;
and
(4)
for sessions,
and is defined inductively by the typing rules in
Section

5.1
.

\par\vskip 3.0pt plus 1.0pt minus 1.0pt\centerline{\hbox{$\begin{array}[]{c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}!\mathtt{read}.{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}?\mathtt{report}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}).{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}\quad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}!\mathtt{trigger}.\sum\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}?\mathtt{fatal}.{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}\\

\par\vskip 3.0pt plus 1.0pt minus 1.0pt\noindent and message queues ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{h}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{h}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{h}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}$,

\par Process ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{C}}}}}}$ (resp. ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{L}}}}}}$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}}$)

${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{I}}}}}\right)}$),
and
