\par\@@numbered@section{appendix}{toc}{Proofs for Section \ref{sec:gtype}}
\par With regard to recursive global types, we define their \emph{unfolding} as
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}\mathord{\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}}}\right\}}}\right)}$, and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}$ otherwise.
A recursive type ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}$ must be guarded (or
contractive), i.e.{} the unfolding leads to a progressive prefix, e.g.{} a
transmission.
Unguarded types, such as ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}}$ and
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}^{\prime}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}}}}$, are excluded.
Similar definitions and requirements apply for local types.
\par\par\@@numbered@section{subsection}{toc}{Live and Crashed Roles}
\begin{lem}If \;${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, \;then \;${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}$ and
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}\subseteq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$.
\end{lem}
\begin{proof}By induction on \autoref{def:gtype:remove-role}. We detail interesting cases
here:
\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@$$\textstyle{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}})}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}})}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}})}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\right\}}}\cup\bigcup\limits_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}}.$$\noindent The required result follows by inductive hypothesis that ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}$, and \linebreak${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}\subseteq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}})}$.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@$${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}})}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}})}$$The required result follows by inductive hypothesis that ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}})}$, and \linebreak${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}})}\subseteq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}})}$.
\par\end{enumerate}The rest of the cases are similar or straightforward.
\end{proof}
\par\begin{lem}If \;${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, \;then\; ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}\setminus\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\right\}}}\subseteq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$.
\end{lem}
\begin{proof}By induction on \autoref{def:gtype:remove-role}. We detail interesting cases here:
\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@$$\textstyle{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}})}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}})}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}})}=\bigcup\limits_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}}.$$\noindent The required result follows by inductive hypothesis that
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}\setminus\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\right\}}}\subseteq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}})}$.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@$${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}})}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}})}$$The required result follows by inductive hypothesis that
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}})}\setminus\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\right\}}}\subseteq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}})}$.
\end{enumerate}The rest of the cases are similar or straightforward.
\end{proof}
\par\begin{lem}If \;${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}$ and\;
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, then
\;${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$.
\end{lem}
\begin{proof}We know that ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}$; otherwise, we may have ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}=\emptyset$, a contradiction
to ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$. By induction on the structure of ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}$:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@Case ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}$:
\par We perform case analysis on ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}$:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$: we have ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})\right\}_{i\in\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{j\in I}\,\middle|\,{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}\right\}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}$: we have ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})\right\}_{i\in I}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}$: we have
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}}$.
Since ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$, and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}$,
there exists $j\in I$ such that ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}})}$. Then, by applying inductive hypothesis,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}$, and therefore, we have
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}}=\linebreak{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}\,\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqcap}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I\setminus\{j\}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$.
\end{itemize}\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}$:
\par We perform case analysis on ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}$:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}$: we have
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}}$.
Since ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$, and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}$,
there exists $k\in I$ such that ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{k}}})}$. Then, by applying inductive hypothesis,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{k}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathsf{end}}$, and therefore, we have
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}}=\linebreak{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{k}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}\,\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqcap}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I\setminus\{k\}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$, as desired. Meanwhile, we obtain that
$\forall l\in I:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{l}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$: we have ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}$, which follows that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$ by the fact that $\forall l\in I:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{l}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}$: we have ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})\right\}_{i\in I}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$.
\end{itemize}\end{itemize}Other cases are similar.
\end{proof}
\par\par\begin{lem}If \;${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$ and\;
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, then
\;${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$.
\end{lem}
\begin{proof}By induction on the structure of ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}$:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@Case ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}$:
since ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, we have ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$,
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}$, and for all $i\in I$, ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}})}$ and
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}})}$ by~\autoref{def:active_crashed_roles}.
Thus, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$ by applying inductive hypothesis
and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\,\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqcap}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}\,=\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}$: since ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, we have
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}$ by~\autoref{def:active_crashed_roles}.
We have two further subcases to consider:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@If $\operatorname{fv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}}\right)\neq\emptyset$, we have ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$ by applying inductive hypothesis.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Otherwise, we have ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$ immediately.
\end{itemize}\end{itemize}Other cases are similar or trivial.
\end{proof}
\par\par
\par\par\@@numbered@section{subsection}{toc}{Subtyping}
\par\begin{lem}For any closed, well-guarded local type ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}$ holds.
\end{lem}
\begin{proof}We construct a relation $R=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T})\right\}}}$.
It is straightforward
that $R$ satisfies all clauses of~\autoref{def:subtyping}.
Hence, since $\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}$ is the largest relation satisfying such rules, $R\subseteq\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}$.
\end{proof}
\par\begin{lem}For any closed, well-guarded local type ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}U}$,
if ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}U}$ hold, then ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}U}$
holds.
\end{lem}
\begin{proof}We construct a relation
$R=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}U})}\,\middle|\,{\exists{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\text{ such that }{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\text{ and }{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}U}}\right\}}}$.
By showing that $R$ satisfies all clauses of~\autoref{def:subtyping}, it follows that $R\subseteq\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}$.
\end{proof}
\par\par See \ref{restatable:propSubtyping}
\begin{proof}Immediate consequence of \autoref{lem:reflexive-subtyping} and~\autoref{transitive-subtyping}.
\end{proof}
\par\par\par\begin{lem}For any closed, well-guarded local type ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}$,
\enumerate*
\par${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}$; and
\par${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right)}$.
\end{lem}
\begin{proof}\enumerate*\par If ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}}}$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}$ holds by ${\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[Sub-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu}$R]}}}$. Otherwise,
by \autoref{lem:reflexive-subtyping}.
\par If ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}}}$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}\right)}$ holds by ${\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[Sub-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu}$L]}}}$. Otherwise,
by \autoref{lem:reflexive-subtyping}.
\end{proof}
\par See \ref{restatable:mergeSubtyping}
\begin{proof}By constructing a relation
$R=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{j}})}\,\middle|\,{j\in I}\right\}}}$, and showing
that $R$ satisfies all clauses of~\autoref{def:subtyping}.
\end{proof}
\par\par See \ref{restatable:mergeUpperSubtyping}
\begin{proof}By constructing a relation $R=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}}})\right\}}}$, and showing
that $R$ satisfies all clauses of~\autoref{def:subtyping}. \end{proof}
\par See \ref{restatable:submergeSubtyping}
\begin{proof}By constructing a relation $R=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}_{i}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}}})\right\}}}$, and showing

\par\par\par
\par\begin{lem}If \; ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$ \; with \; ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$ \;
and \; ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\notin{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$, \;
then \;
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}})}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}$.
\end{lem}
\begin{proof}We construct a relation
$R=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}})}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}\,\middle|\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathcal{R}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\in{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\right\}}}$, and show that $R\subseteq\;\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}$.
Consider all possible shapes of ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}$:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@Case ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}$:
\par We perform case analysis on the role being projected upon:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@On LHS,
we have ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})\right\}_{i\in\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{j\in I}\,\middle|\,{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}\right\}}}}}$.
\par On RHS, we perform case analysis on the role being removed:
\par\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@we have
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}})}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}$, and thus
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}})}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}=$ \linebreak${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}})}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})\right\}_{i\in\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{j\in I}\,\middle|\,{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}\right\}}}}}$,
apply {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[Sub-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}$]}}} and coinductive
hypothesis.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@(${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$) we have

\end{proof}
\par\begin{lem}~\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@If
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}$, then
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{j}}\right\}_{j\in J}}$, and $J\subseteq I$,
and $\forall i\in J:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}$.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@If
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}$, then
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{j}}\right\}_{j\in J}}$, and $I\subseteq J$,
and $\forall i\in I:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}$.
\end{enumerate}\end{lem}
\begin{proof}By \autoref{lem:unfold-subtyping}, the transitivity of subtyping, and \autoref{def:subtyping} ({\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[Sub-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}$]}}}, {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[Sub-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}$]}}}).
\end{proof}

\par See \ref{restatable:lemNorevival}
\begin{proof}\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@By induction on global type reductions: since ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}$, we start from {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[GR-{$\mu$}]}}}.
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@Case {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[GR-{$\mu$}]}}}:
we have
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}}$ and
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}{}\mathord{\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}}}\right\}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$
by {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[GR-{$\mu$}]}}} and its inversion.
Hence, by $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}{}\mathord{\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}}}\right\}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$, ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}$, and inductive hypothesis,
we have ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}{}\mathord{\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}}}\right\}}})}$.
Therefore,
by ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}{}\mathord{\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}}}\right\}}})}$,
we conclude with ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, as desired.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[GR-{$\&$}]}}}:
we have ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{j}}$ by {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[GR-{$\&$}]}}}. It follows that ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}=\bigcup\limits_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}})}}$
and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{j}}})}$ with $j\in I$, and hence, ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}\subseteq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$.
Therefore, by ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}$,
we conclude with ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, as desired.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[GR-{Ctx-i}]}}}: we have ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}$, ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}$, $\forall i\in I:\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}\rangle$, and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subj}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}})}\notin\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\right\}}}$
by {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[GR-{Ctx-i}]}}} and its inversion. It follows that
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}=\bigcup\limits_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}})}}$, ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}=\bigcup\limits_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{i}}})}}$,
and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}$. Then by $\forall i\in I:\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}\rangle$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}$, and inductive hypothesis, we have $\forall i\in I:\text{if }{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{i}}})},\text{then }{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}})}$.
Therefore, by ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in\bigcup\limits_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{i}}})}}$, we conclude with ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in\bigcup\limits_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}})}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, as desired.
\end{itemize}Other cases are similar.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@Similar to the proof of (1).
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@The proof is trivial by {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[GR-{$\lightning$}]}}} and its inversion.
\end{enumerate}\end{proof}
\par See \ref{restatable:lemWellAnnoPreserve}
\begin{proof}By induction on global type reductions:
\par\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@Case {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[GR-{$\lightning$}]}}}: we have ${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}\cup\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\right\}}}$, ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}$, ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$,
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, and ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}$ by {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[GR-{$\lightning$}]}}} and its inversion.
\par\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{item:wa:reliable-no-crash}:
from the premise, we have
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}\cap{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}=\emptyset$. Since ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, by \autoref{lem:gtype:crashed-crash-remove-role},
we have ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}\setminus\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\right\}}}\subseteq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$. Then we consider two cases:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@if ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}$, then
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\right\}}}\cup({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}\setminus\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\right\}}})$. Hence, by ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$,
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}\setminus\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\right\}}}\subseteq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}\cap{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}=\emptyset$,
we have ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}\cap{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}=\emptyset$.
