\par\begin{lem}Given a local type ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}$, which is a subtype of projection from a global type
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}$ on a role ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}$ with respect to a set of reliable roles ${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$,
i.e.{} ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})$,
then:
\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@If\;
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}$, then either
\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$,
where
$I\subseteq I^{\prime}$, and
for all $i\in I$:
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}$, \linebreak${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}^{\prime}_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})$,
and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}$;
or,
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}}}\right\}}_{j\in J}}$,
or
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t}}}}}}{:}{k}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}}}\right\}}_{j\in J}}$,
where for all $j\in J$:
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})$,
with ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t}}}}}$.
\end{enumerate}\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@If\;
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}$, then either
\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$,
or
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$,
where \linebreak$I^{\prime}\subseteq I$, and
for all $i\in I^{\prime}$:
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}$,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}^{\prime}_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})$,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}$,
and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\notin{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$ implies $\exists k\in I^{\prime}:{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{k}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$;
or,
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}}}\right\}}_{j\in J}}$,
or
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t}}}}}}{:}{k}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}}}\right\}}_{j\in J}}$,
where for all $j\in J$:
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})$,
with ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t}}}}}$.
\end{enumerate}\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@If\;
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$,
then ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$.
\end{enumerate}\end{lem}
\begin{proof}By the definition of global type projection~(\autoref{def:global-proj}).
\end{proof}
\par\par\par\begin{lem}If role ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$ is unreliable, ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\notin{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$, and a global type
projects onto ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}$ with an external choice from ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$,\linebreak${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{S}^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}$, then there must be a crash handling branch,
$\exists j\in I:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$.
\end{lem}
\begin{proof}By induction on \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{item:proj-inv:recv} of \autoref{lem:inv-proj}.
\end{proof}
\par\begin{lem}If a local type ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}$ is a subtype of an external choice with a matching role, obtained
via projection from a global type ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}$, i.e.,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}$ is of the form
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}}}\right\}}_{j\in J}}$ or
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t}}}}}}{:}{k}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}}}\right\}}_{j\in J}}$, and a queue environment ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ is associated
with $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle$, then there exists a global type $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$ and a queue environment ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ such that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\right)}$ is of the form
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$ or
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$, and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ is
associated with $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$ with ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}$.
\end{lem}
\begin{proof}Apply \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{item:proj-inv:recv} of \autoref{lem:inv-proj} on the premise, we have $\forall j\in J:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}$ with ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t}}}}}$, which follows that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{j\in J}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}}$.
Then, by \autoref{lem:merge-subtyping}, we get $\forall j\in J:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}$.
We take an arbitrary ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}$ with $j\in J$. By applying \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{item:proj-inv:recv} of \autoref{lem:inv-proj} again,
we have two cases.
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@Case (1):
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{l}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$ or
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$.
\par Since ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}$,
we only need to show that there exists ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ such that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ is associated with $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\rangle$ and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}$. We consider two subcases:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}}}\right\}}_{j\in J}}$: by~\autoref{def:assoc-queue}, we have that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ is associated with ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}$. We take ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ as ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t}}}}}}{:}{k}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}}}\right\}}_{j\in J}}$, we perform case analysis on ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{k}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$ and
\linebreak${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{k}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{k}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$: by~\autoref{def:assoc-queue}, we have that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ is associated with $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\rangle$.
We take ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ as ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{k}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$: by~\autoref{def:assoc-queue}, we have that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{k}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{k}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}}}$ and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}]}$ is associated with
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\rangle$. We take ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}]}$. Since ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}$
and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t}}}}}$, it is straightforward that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}$, as required.
\end{itemize}\end{itemize}\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case (2): ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{u^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{l}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{l}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{l}}}}}\right\}}_{l\in L}}$ or
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{u}}}}}}{:}{k}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{l}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{l}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{l}}}}}\right\}}_{l\in L}}$.
\par We can construct a queue environment ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ as in case (1), which is associated with $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\rangle$.
The thesis is then proved by applying inductive hypothesis on $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\rangle$ and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$.
\end{itemize}\end{proof}
\par\par
\par\begin{lem}If ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ is associated with $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle$ and
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, then
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}$ is associated with
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}\cup\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\right\}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}\rangle$.
\end{lem}
\par\begin{proof}We denote ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}$ in the
subsequent proof.
To show association, there are two parts: namely a shape-dependent
part, and a shape-independent part.
\par We first show shape-independent part, which shared for all cases:
that a crashed role ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}$ is in ${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}\cup\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\right\}}}$ iff ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}$.
It follows that the roles ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\in{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}$ have the requirements
satisfied from the premise, and we set ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}$, and that ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}$ is in the new set of crashed
roles.
\par Shape-dependent part are by induction on the definition of
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}$:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@Case
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}})}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}})}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}$ where $j\in I$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$.
\par Since ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ is associated with $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle$, we that
$\forall i\in I:$ ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ is associated with
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{i}}}\rangle$, and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}$.
\par By inductive hypothesis, we have ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ is associated with
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}})}\rangle$.
Moreover, since ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$, we can show that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}$.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}})}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}.$
\par Subcase ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$:
\par By inductive hypothesis, we know ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ is associated with
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}\cup\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\right\}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{j}}}\rangle$, as
required.
\par Subcase ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$:
\par From association, we have
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{j}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}}}$
and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}]}$
is associated with
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{j}}}\rangle$.
\par By inductive hypothesis, we know ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}]}{}[\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}$
is associated with
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}\cup\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\right\}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{j}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}\rangle$.
\par Since
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}]}{}[\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$,
so ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ is associated with $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}\cup\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\right\}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{j}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}\rangle$.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}})}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}$, where $j\in I$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$.
\par Since ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ is associated with $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle$, we
have that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ is associated with
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\rangle$.
\par By inductive hypothesis, we have ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ is associated with
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}\cup\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\right\}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}\rangle$, as required.
\par Other cases follows directly by inductive hypothesis or trivially.
\end{itemize}\end{proof}
\par\begin{lem}If\,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$,\,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}$,\,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{1}}}\rangle}}$,\,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subj}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}})}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subj}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}})}}\right)}$,\,
and\,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{1}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subj}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}})}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subj}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}})}}\right)}]}$,\,
then\,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$.
\end{lem}
\begin{proof}By induction on reductions of configuration.
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@Case {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}$]}}}: ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}={{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{k}}})}}$.
We apply and invert {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}$]}}} on
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ to get
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}$,
$k\in I$,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{k}}]}$, and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{k}}})}}}]}$.
Then by applying {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}$]}}} on
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}$ and
$k\in I$, we obtain that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{k}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$, which follows that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$, as desired.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}$]}}}: ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}={{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{k}}})}}$.
We apply and invert {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}$]}}} on
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ to get
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}$,
$k\in I$,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{k}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}}}$,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{k}}]}$, and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}]}$.
Then by applying {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}$]}}} on
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}$,
$k\in I$, and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{k}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}}}$,
we obtain that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{k}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$, which follows that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$, as desired.
\par\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-$\lightning$]}}}: ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}={{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}$.
We apply and invert {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-$\lightning$]}}} on
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ to get
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}]}$, and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}$.
Then by applying {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-$\lightning$]}}} on
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$ and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$,
we obtain that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$, which follows that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$, as desired.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-$\odot$]}}}: ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}={{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathord{\odot}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}$.
We apply and invert {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-$\odot$]}}} on
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ to get
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}$,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$,
$k\in I$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}$, and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{k}}]}$.
Since ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}$ and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$, it holds that ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}$. Then by
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{1}}}\rangle}}$,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$.
We apply {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-$\odot$]}}} on
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}$,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$,
$k\in I$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$, and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}$ to obtain
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{k}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$, which follows that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$, as desired.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-$\mu$]}}}: by inductive hypothesis.
\end{itemize}\end{proof}
\par\par\par We define
${I}^{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}\setminus{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}$ to be an index set with the special label
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$ removed, i.e.{} ${I}^{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}\setminus{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{i\in I}\,\middle|\,{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}\right\}}}$.
\par See \ref{restatable:thmProjSoundness}
\begin{proof}By induction on reductions of global type
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$.
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@Case {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[GR-{$\lightning$}]}}}:
\par From the premise we have
\@@amsgather
\par Let ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}]}$.
We show
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}\rangle}}$\;
and
\;${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$.
\par For the first part:
By association~\eqref{eq:soundness:movecrash_assoc_pre}, we know that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}$.
By ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}$, ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$,
and~\autoref{lem:in-roles-not-end}, we know that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$, which gives ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$.
By ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$, we also know that ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}$, which gives
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$.
Thus we apply {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-$\lightning$]}}} to obtain the thesis.
\par For the second part:
\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@From association~\eqref{eq:soundness:movecrash_assoc_pre}, we know
that
$\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}$.
\par By~\autoref{lem:gtype:crash-remove-role}, we know
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}\subseteq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$.
\par For any ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}$,
we apply~\autoref{lem:proj-non-crashing-role-preserve}, to obtain
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}})}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}$
\par Thus we have, by transitivity of subtyping,
$\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\lightning}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}})}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}$, as required.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@From association~\eqref{eq:soundness:movecrash_assoc_pre}, we know
that
$\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\in{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$,
and they are unchanged in ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}$.
\par Moreover, we have updated ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$.
\par This completes the consideration of the set ${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}$ \eqref{eq:soundness:movecrash_rolesCi}.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@No change here.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@By~\autoref{lem:queue-assoc-crash}.
\end{enumerate}\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[GR-{$\mu$}]}}}:
\par From the premise we have
\@@amsgather
\par By~\autoref{prop:gt-lts-unfold-once}, we have
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{0}}{}\mathord{\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{0}}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}}}\right\}}}\rangle}}$, and we can apply inductive hypothesis to obtain the desired result.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[GR-{$\oplus$}]}}}:
\par From the premise we have
\@@amsgather
By association \eqref{eq:soundness:moveout_assoc_pre} and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}$,
we know that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}=$ \linebreak${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}})\right\}_{i\in{I}^{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}\setminus{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}}}$. Then by~\autoref{lem:subtyping-invert}, we obtain that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}=$ \linebreak${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$, where $I^{\prime}\subseteq{I}^{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}\setminus{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}}$ and $\forall i\in I^{\prime}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}$.
Note that here for any $i\in I^{\prime}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}$.
\par Since the ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$ label cannot appear in the internal choices, it holds that for any $i\in I^{\prime}$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$.
Therefore, with $\forall i\in I^{\prime}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}$, we can set $j\in I^{\prime}$ with ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$ and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}={{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{j}}})}}={{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{j}}})}}$.
\par Let ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{j}}]};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{j}}})}}}]}$.
We show that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$\;
and
\;${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle}}$.
\par For the first part: we apply {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}$]}}} on those we have:
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$ and $j\in I^{\prime}$, to obtain the thesis.
\par For the second part:
\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@We want to show $\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}})}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}}$. We consider three subcases:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}$: since
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{j}}$, $j\in I^{\prime}$, and $\forall i\in I^{\prime}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}$,
we have ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}$, as desired.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$:
by association~\eqref{eq:soundness:moveout_assoc_pre},
we have that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}=$ \linebreak${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}})\right\}_{i\in I}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}$, as desired.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}$:
