\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$: with ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{k}}})}$, we have ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}$, and hence,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I^{\prime}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}}}}$.
Furthermore, by \eqref{eq:stenvp-crash-detect-type-cxt-cons} and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}$,
it holds that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I^{\prime}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}}}}$.
Then, by~\autoref{lem:merge-subtyping} and transitivity of subtyping, we can conclude that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{k}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}}}}$, as desired.
\end{itemize}\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@Trivial by ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}$.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@Trivial by ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}$ and the
fact that if ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{k}}})}$, then ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$:
with ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{k}}})}$, by~\autoref{lem:not-in-roles-end}, we have ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{k}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$.
Furthermore, by \eqref{eq:stenvp-crash-detect-global-label-equvi}, it holds that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$, and thus,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$, as desired.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@Since ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ is associated with $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}}\rangle$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$, by~\autoref{def:assoc-queue}, we have that
$\forall i\in I^{\prime}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\text{~is associated with~}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}\rangle$, which follows that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ is associated with
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{k}}}\rangle$, as required.
\end{enumerate}\par\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case (2): similar to the case (2) in Case ${\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}$]}}}$.
\end{itemize}\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case ${\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-$\mu$]}}}$:
\par By induction hypothesis and~\autoref{prop:gt-lts-unfold}.
\end{itemize}\end{proof}
\par\par\par\@@numbered@section{subsection}{toc}{Safety by Projection}
\par\par\par\begin{lem}If \;${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}$,\;
then\; ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ \;is\; ${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$-safe.\end{lem}
\par\begin{proof}Let $\varphi=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}}\,\middle|\,{\exists{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}:\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}^{\!*}{}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle\text{ and }{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle}}}\right\}}}$.
Take any ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\in\varphi$, we show that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ satisfies all
clauses in~\autoref{def:mpst-env-safe}, which means that $\varphi$ is an ${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$-safety
property. Then we can conclude that, since $\varphi\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\right)$ holds,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ is ${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$-safe.
\par By definition of $\varphi$, there exists $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$ with
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}^{\!*}{}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$ and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle}}$.
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[S-${\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}}{\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}}$]}}}:
from the premise, we have
\@@amsgather
Apply~\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{item:proj-inv:recv} of~\autoref{lem:inv-proj} on \eqref{eq:stenvp-safety-rec-comp},
we have two cases.
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@Case (1):
\@@amsgather
First, we show that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\right)}$ cannot be the form of
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$. We prove this by contradiction on two subcases:
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}$:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$: by association \eqref{eq:safety-assoc-1}, we have
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}$, a desired contradiction to \eqref{eq:safety-queue-not-empty}.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}$: we have ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\in{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}$. Hence, by association \eqref{eq:safety-assoc-1}, we have
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}$, a desired contradiction to \eqref{eq:safety-queue-available}.
\end{itemize}\par It follows that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$. We are left to show that $j\in I$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{j}}$, and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}}}$, and then by applying
{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}$]}}}, we can conclude with ${{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{j}}})}}}}}$.
We consider two subcases: ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$: by association,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}$, a desired contradiction to \eqref{eq:safety-queue-not-empty}.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$: by association, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}}}$. Moreover, with \eqref{eq:safety-subset} and \eqref{eq:stenvp-safety-receive-label-equvi},
we obtain that $j\in I$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{j}}$, and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}$, which follows that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}}}$, as desired.
\end{itemize}\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case (2): ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}}}\right\}}_{j\in J}}$,
or
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t}}}}}}{:}{k}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}}}\right\}}_{j\in J}}$,
where for all $j\in J$:
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{j}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}})$,
with ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}}}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t}}}}}$.
\par We apply~\autoref{lem:eventual-global-form} to get that there exists a global type
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}\rangle$ and a queue environment ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime\prime}}$ such that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}\right)}$ is of the form
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$ or
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$, and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime\prime}}$ is
associated with $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}\rangle$ with ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}$.
The following proof argument is similar to that for Case (1).
\end{itemize}\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[S-${{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}{\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}}$]}}}: from the premise, we have
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$, and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}$.
We just need to show that there exists $k\in I$ with ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$, and then by applying
{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-$\odot$]}}}, we can conclude with
${{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathord{\odot}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}}}$.
By the association that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle}}$,
we have ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}$. Moreover, with ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$, which follows directly from
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$, we can apply~\autoref{lem:crash-lab-exists} to
get $\exists k\in I:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$, as desired.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[S-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu}$]}}}: let ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime\prime}}$ be constructed from ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ with
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}\mathord{\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}}}\right\}}]}$ and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$.
We want to show that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle}}$.
From the premise, we have ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}}}$.
Then by association that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle}}$,
it holds that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}$. Hence, by inverting {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[Sub-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu}$L]}}}, we have
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}{}\mathord{\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}}}\right\}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\upharpoonright_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}$. Therefore, by~\autoref{def:assoc}, we conclude with
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle}}$, as desired.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[S-$\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to_{\!\lightning}}}$]}}}: from the premise, we have ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime\prime}}$.
Hence, by~\autoref{thm:gtype:proj-comp}, there exists $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}\rangle$ with
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}\rangle$, where ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}$ for all ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$, and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}\rangle}}$.
By definition of $\varphi$, the configuration after transition ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime\prime}}$ is in $\varphi$, as desired.
\end{itemize}\end{proof}
\par\par\par\@@numbered@section{subsection}{toc}{Deadlock Freedom by Projection}
\begin{lem}If \;${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}$,\;
then\; ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ \;is\; ${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$-deadlock-free.\end{lem}
\par\begin{proof}Since ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}$,
by \autoref{lem:ext-proj-safe}, we have that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ is ${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$-safe.
By operational correspondence of global type $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle$
and configuration ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$~(\autoref{thm:gtype:proj-comp} and~\autoref{thm:gtype:proj-sound}),
there exists
a global type $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$ such that
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\!\xrightarrow{}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}^{\!*}\!\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle\!\not\xrightarrow{}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}$,
with associated configurations
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to^{*}_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\not\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}$. Since no further reductions are possible for the global type,
the global type $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$ must be of the form $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}\rangle$.
By applying~\autoref{def:assoc} on ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}\rangle}}$,
we have
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}$, where
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}}}\right)}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\,\middle|\,{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}\right\}}}$ and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\,\middle|\,{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}}\right\}}}$, as required.
By association again, we have that,
for any ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$, if ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\in{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}(={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}}\right)}),{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}$, and otherwise, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}$, as required.
\end{proof}
\par\par\par\par\@@numbered@section{subsection}{toc}{Liveness by Projection}
\begin{lem}If \;${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}$,\;
then\; ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ \;is\; ${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$-live.\end{lem}
\begin{proof}Since ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}}$,
by~\autoref{lem:ext-proj-safe}, we have that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ is ${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$-safe.
\par We are left to
show that if ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to^{*}_{\!{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$, then any non-crashing path starting with ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$ which is fair is also live.
By operational correspondence of global type $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle$
and configuration ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta}$ (\autoref{thm:gtype:proj-comp} and~\autoref{thm:gtype:proj-sound}),
there exists
a global type $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$ such that
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\!\xrightarrow{}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}^{\!*}\!\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$ and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle}}$.
We construct a non-crashing fair path $({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}})_{n\in N}$,
where $N=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{0,1,2,\ldots\right\}}}$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{0}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}$,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{0}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}$, and $\forall n\in N$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n+1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n+1}}$. Then we just need to show that $({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}})_{n\in N}$
is live.
\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@Suppose that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$. By operational correspondence
of $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$ and configuration ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{0}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{0}}$, and
$\forall n\in N$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n+1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n+1}}$, there
exists $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\mathscr{C}}^{\prime}_{n}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{n}}}\rangle$ such that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\mathscr{C}}^{\prime}_{n}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{n}}}\rangle}}$.
By~\autoref{lem:eventual-global-form} and~\autoref{def:assoc}, we only need to consider
the case that
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{n}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}$ with
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}$.
By applying {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[GR-{$\&$}]}}},
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\mathscr{C}}^{\prime}_{n}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{n}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{j}}})}}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\mathscr{C}}^{\prime}_{n}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{j}}}\rangle$. Hence, by the soundness of association, it holds
that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}}}}}$.
Finally, combining the fact that $({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}})_{n\in N}$ is fair with ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}}}}}$, we can conclude that there exists $k$ such that $n\leq k\in N$ and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{k}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}})}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{k+1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{k+1}}$, as desired.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@Suppose that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}$. By operational correspondence
of $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$ and configuration ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{0}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{0}}$, and
$\forall n\in N$, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n+1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n+1}}$, there
exists $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\mathscr{C}}^{\prime}_{n}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{n}}}\rangle$ such that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}{\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\mathscr{C}}^{\prime}_{n}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{n}}}\rangle}}$.
By~\autoref{def:assoc}, \autoref{lem:inv-proj}, and~\autoref{lem:eventual-global-form},
we only have to consider that ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{n}}$ is of the form
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$
or
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$,
where
$I^{\prime}\subseteq I$, and
for all $i\in I^{\prime}$:
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}$,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}$,
and ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\notin{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}$ implies $\exists k\in I^{\prime}:{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{k}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$.
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{n}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$: we first show that
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}$. Since ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$, by~\autoref{def:assoc}, we have that ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\notin{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\mathscr{C}}^{\prime}_{n}}}}}$, and hence, ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}$. Given that ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{n}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$, by association, \autoref{def:assoc}, and inversion of subtyping,
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}$ is of the form
${\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I^{\prime\prime}}}$ where
$I^{\prime\prime}\subseteq I^{\prime}$. Then applying {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}$]}}},
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}}\,\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})}}}}}$ for some $j\in I^{\prime\prime}$.
Then together with the fairness of $({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}})_{n\in N}$,
we have that there exists $k,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m^{\prime}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime\prime}}$ such that $n\leq k\in N$ and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{k}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m^{\prime}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime\prime}}})}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{k+1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{k+1}}$,
which follows that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{k+1}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{k}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{k}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m^{\prime}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime\prime}}})}}}]}$. Finally, by the previous case (L1), we can conclude that there exists $k^{\prime},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m^{\prime\prime}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime\prime\prime}}$ such that $k+1\leq k^{\prime}\in N$ and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{k^{\prime}}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{k^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m^{\prime\prime}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{k^{\prime}+1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{k^{\prime}+1}}$, as desired.
\par\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{n}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{i}}})\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I^{\prime}}}$: we consider two subcases:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$: by applying {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[GR-{$\&$}]}}},
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\mathscr{C}}^{\prime}_{n}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime}_{n}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})}}}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\mathscr{C}}^{\prime}_{n}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{G}^{\prime\prime}_{j}}}\rangle$.
Hence, by the soundness association, we have that ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}}\,\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})}}}}}$.
Finally, combining the fact that $({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}})_{n\in N}$ is fair with
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})}}}}}$,
we can conclude that there exists $k$ such that $n\leq k\in N$ and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{k}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}:{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B^{\prime}_{j}}})}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{k+1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{k+1}}$, as desired.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$: consider the following two subcases:
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}$:
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$ by ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\in{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\mathscr{C}}^{\prime}_{n}}}}}$. By~\autoref{def:assoc}, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}$. Now applying {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}$-$\odot$]}}} on ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B_{i}}})}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{T}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}$ and
${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}j}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$ with $j\in I^{\prime}\subseteq I$, we have that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathord{\odot}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}}$. Then together with the fairness of
$({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{n}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{n}})_{n\in N}$ , we can conclude that there exists $k$ such that $n\leq k\in N$ and
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{k}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathord{\odot}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{k+1}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Delta}^{\prime}_{k+1}}$.
\par\par\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\lightning}}}}}}}$: since ${\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{\mathsf{crash}}}$, together with $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\!\xrightarrow{}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}^{\!*}\!\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$, we know that ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q^{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\dagger}}}}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$ holds, a desired contradiction.
\end{itemize}\end{itemize}\end{itemize}\end{enumerate}\end{proof}

\par\begin{lem}If $\Theta,x:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}$ and $\Theta\vdash{v}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}$,
then
$\Theta\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\{{v}/x\}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}$.
\end{lem}
\begin{proof}By induction on the structure of ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}$.
\end{proof}
\par\begin{lem}If $\emptyset\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}\downarrow{v}$, then $\emptyset\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}v}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}$.
\end{lem}
\begin{proof}By induction on the derivation of $\emptyset\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}e}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}B}$.
\end{proof}

\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[r-cond-F]}}}: similar to the case {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[r-cond-T]}}}.

\@@amsgather

we have that $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathbb{M}}_{1}}$. By induction hypothesis, either

$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathbb{M}}^{\prime}_{1}}$. Now by \eqref{eq:r_struct_eq_3} and~\eqref{lem:cong} of \autoref{lem:typingcongruence}, we have that either $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}^{\prime}}$ or
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}^{\prime}}$, as desired.
\end{itemize}\end{proof}

\par See \ref{restatable:lemSessionDF}
\begin{proof}\par Assume that $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}}\mathrel{\to_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}^{*}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}^{\prime}}\mathrel{\not{\!\!\to_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}}}$, we need to prove that either
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}^{\prime}}\Rrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\triangleleft{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\triangleleft{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}$ for some ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}$, or ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}^{\prime}}\Rrightarrow\prod_{i\in I}({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}_{i}}\triangleleft{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lightning}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}_{i}}\triangleleft{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\oslash})$ with $I\neq\emptyset$.
\par\par\par\par By applying~\autoref{lem:sr} (subject reduction) repeatedly as needed, there exists a global type
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$ such that
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\!\xrightarrow{}_{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{R}}}}}}^{\!*}\!\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$ and $\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}^{\prime}}$.
Since no further reductions are possible for
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}^{\prime}}$, by~\autoref{lem:sf} (session fidelity),
the global type
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$ cannot be reduced further either, implying that
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle$ must be of the form
$\langle{{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\mathscr{C}^{\prime}}}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}\rangle$.
\par Furthermore, using~{\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[t-sess]}}} and the proof for~\autoref{lem:proj:df}, we can conclude that
${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}^{\prime}}$ is of the form $\prod_{i\in I}({\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}_{i}}\triangleleft{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}_{i}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}_{i}}\triangleleft{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{h}^{\prime}_{i}})$
such that $\forall i\in I:\,\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}_{i}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}_{i}}}\right)}$,
$\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{h}^{\prime}_{i}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}_{i}}}\right)}$, where ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}$, with
$\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}}}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{end}}$,
$\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathsf{stop}}$, and
for any ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}$, if ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\Gamma}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\lightning}}}}\right)},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\cdot,{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}$, and otherwise, ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Delta^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\epsilon}$.
\par Then, by applying {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[t-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}$]}}}, {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[t-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\oslash}$]}}}, {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[t-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lightning}$]}}}, {\color[rgb]{0.1,0.3,0.1}\text{{\scriptsize[t-${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}$]}}},
and the precongruence rules
${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\triangleleft{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\triangleleft{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}}\Rrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}}\Rrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}^{\prime}}\text{ and }{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}^{\prime}}\Rrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}^{\prime\prime}}\implies{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}}\Rrightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{M}^{\prime\prime}}$, the thesis follows.
\end{proof}
\par\par\par\par\par\par\par\par\par See \ref{restatable:lemSessionLive}
\begin{proof}\par The proof follows the same structure as~\autoref{lem:session_deadlock_free}, but instead uses the definition of live sessions~(\autoref{def:session_live}), the proof for~\autoref{lem:ext-proj-live}, and the relevant typing rules in~\lx@cref{creftypecap\lx@tilde refnum}{fig:processes:typesystem}.
\end{proof}
